- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Rain Slime Shard
Categories: Uncommon items, Shards
[Rain Slime Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Shard ID: U12
Families: None
Skill: Combat
Category: Combat
[Attribute]
Name: Mana Steal ([[Attributes]])
Effect: Regain +0.5–5 Mana every time you hit a monster. Cooldown: 0.5s.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: RAIN_SLIME
Tooltip: Mana Steal I Regain +0.5 Mana every time you hit a monster.; Cooldown: 0.5s; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; UNCOMMON COMBAT SHARD (ID U12)
The Rain Slime Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Mana Steal]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Rain Slime Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Rain Slime]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Rain Slime Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Rain Slime Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mana Steal]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mana Steal ([[Attributes]]); Regain +0.5–5 Mana every time you hit a monster. Cooldown: 0.5s.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Rain Slime Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Rain Slime Shard has the following description:
- Rain Slimes appear during heavy downpours, their translucent bodies shimmering with absorbed raindrops.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Rain Slime Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Rain Slime.|
|Aug 5, 2025|0.23.3|Renamed Rain Slime to Rain Slime Shard.|

# Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear
Aliases: Reheated Gummy Polar Bear, Smoldering Polarization
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Crafting ingredients, Chili Pepper Collection
[Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Chili Pepper I (1) ([[Chili Pepper]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✔ Yes
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 4x [[Chili Pepper]] 640x (10 stacks (640 items total)) [[Slimeball]] 2,560x (40 stacks (2,560 items total)) [[Snowball]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REHEATED_GUMMY_POLAR_BEAR
Tooltip: Consumable; Grants the Smoldering Polarization effect. Consuming multiple stacks the duration.; Smoldering Polarization I (1:00:00); Grants +10☯ Combat Wisdom against Blazes and pacifies nearby Blazes while fighting Inferno Demonlord.; Right-click to consume!; X Rift-Transferable X UNCOMMON
The Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that grants the Smoldering Polarization effect.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Chili Pepper I (1) ([[Chili Pepper]])|4x [[Chili Pepper]] 4x [[Enchanted Snow Block]] 16x [[Enchanted Slimeball]]|Recipe: [[Chili Pepper]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Slimeball]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Snow Block]] x4 → Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear|
## Usage
The Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear can be consumed to gain the Smoldering Polarization effect for 1 hour. It grants +10 [[Combat Wisdom]] from Blazes and makes nearby Blazes passive when fighting [[Inferno Demonlord|Inferno Demonlords]].
With Smoldering Polarization active, most slayer weapons will gain the tooltip showing it will grant +X [[Magic Find]] towards the mob the slayer weapon is made to kill. This is not shown on the [[Pooch Sword]], the [[Shaman Sword]], or the [[Revenant Falchion]], however they will still receive the benefits of [[Habanero Tactics]] if the user has the enchantment. The [[Voidwalker Katana]], despite arguably being a slayer weapon, is not affected by this.
With [[Habanero Tactics]] applied to [[Armor]], the player gains +10-15 [[Magic Find]] on dedicated Slayer weapons.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear]]|Vampire Slayer III (3) ([[Vampire Slayer]])|8x [[Bloodbadge]] 16x [[Bacte Fragment]] 16x [[Nearly-Whole Carrot]] [[Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear]] 16x Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear|Recipe: [[Nearly-Whole Carrot]] x4 ×4 + [[Bacte Fragment]] x8 ×2 + [[Bloodbadge]] x4 ×2 + Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear x16 → [[Perma-Jelled Garlic-Flavored Re-Heated Gummy Polar Bear]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 15, 2026|The immunity effect now lasts for 10 seconds after killing the [[Inferno Demonlord]] boss.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 5, 2022|Added Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Re-heated Gummy Polar Bear to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|The immunity effect now lasts for 10 seconds after killing the [[Inferno Demonlord]] boss.|

The Recluse Fang grants a +150% [[Damage]] bonus against [[Arthropod]] mobs. Its Webcutter ability allows it to clear webs left by the [[Tarantula Broodfather]]'s Web of Lies ability.

The next time there's a spider in your house, we hope you won't be scared to SQUASH IT!

Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Jerry pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; UNCOMMON COSMETIC
The Red Elf Jerry Skin is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Red Elf Jerry Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 25, 2020. Being [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 500 purchases.
## Usage
The Red Elf Jerry Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Jerry Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

The Red Panda Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Visitor Plant]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Red Panda Shard can be purchase from [[Sanger]] for 250,000 [[Coins]]. The player can purchase up to 10 per day.
### Fusion
A Red Panda Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Red Panda Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Visitor Plant]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Visitor Plant ([[Attributes]]); Garden Visitors ([[The Garden]]) have a +0.25%–2.5% chance to bring [[Plant Matter]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

A Red Panda Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

# Repelling Candle
Aliases: Aqua Repelling Candle, Black Repelling Candle, Blue Repelling Candle, Brown Repelling Candle, Candle, Cyan Repelling Candle, Gray Repelling Candle, Green Repelling Candle, Lilac Repelling Candle, Orange Repelling Candle, Pink Repelling Candle, Purple Repelling Candle, Red Repelling Candle, White Repelling Candle, Yellow Repelling Candle
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Pages with UIs, Acacia Log Collection, Blocks
[Repelling Candle]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Acacia Log VII (7) ([[Acacia Log]])
Tooltip Text: Prevents unwanted monsters from spawning on your island!; Emits life-scarring aroma!
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 960x (15 stacks (960 items total)) [[Acacia Log]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Coal]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: REPELLING_CANDLE
Tooltip: Prevents unwanted monsters from spawning on your island!; Emits life-scarring aroma!; UNCOMMON
The Repelling Candle is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be used to prevent [[Mob|Mobs]] from spawning on the player's [[Private Island]]. The crafting recipe for this item is unlocked at [[Acacia Log|Acacia Log VII (7)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Acacia Log VII (7) ([[Acacia Log]])|1x [[Enchanted Coal]] 6x [[Enchanted Acacia Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Coal]] + [[Enchanted Acacia Log]] ×6 → Repelling Candle|
## Usage
When the Repelling Candle is unlocked in the Island Properties menu, [[Mob|monsters]] can be prevented from spawning on the island. Mobs spawned from minions are unaffected by this item.
It is very useful if the player wishes to cull mobs but also leave the island's biome unaltered, as previous solutions would often use a [[Biome Stick|Nether Biome Stick]] to make the hostile mobs into [[Chicken|Chickens]].
## Chat Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Placed on [[The Garden]]|You can't place that here! Everyone is friendly!|
## Gallery
- A placed Repelling Candle
- As seen when placed by 12Minikloon
- A Repelling Candle placed in the Hub
### Colors
- Red
- Orange
- Yellow
- Green
- Blue
- Purple
- Black
- Pink
- Lilac
- Aqua
- Cyan
- Brown
- Gray
- White
## Trivia
- There appears to be a Red Repelling Candle in [[Catacombs Entrance]] in the [[Coal Mine]].
- There is a bug where the Repelling Candle is able to be placed in the Hub. The bug happens if the player clicks on a crafting table with the Repelling Candle in their hand.
- A visual of the candle's sphere can be seen by using mods such as NotEnoughUpdates. The commands to see it are:
  - /neuzeesphere on
  - /neuzeesphere 50 (or 10)
  - /neuzeesphere setcenter - moves center of sphere to current location, so stand on the candle
  - /neuzeesphere off - to remove the sphere
- Unlike most other related blocks, it can still be placed after the 0.19.9 update, but it can also be used from the Island Properties menu without placing.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 2, 2020|0.7.9|Added Repelling Candle.|
|Oct 2, 2020|0.9.5|Fixed bugs when placing or breaking Repelling Candles.|
|Nov 8, 2021|Repelling Candles can no longer be placed on some blocks in the Hub ([[Hub Island]]).|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Repelling Candle now requires Acacia Log VI (6) ([[Acacia Log]]) to craft instead of IX.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Changed Repelling Candle from a placed block to a [[Settings]] feature.|
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Repelling Candle now requires Acacia Log VII (7) ([[Acacia Log]]) to craft instead of VI.|

Revenant Flesh is obtained by killing the [[Revenant Horror]]. It is a [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] drop, and is used to make items like [[Revenant Viscera]], which is used to make many high level items.
## Obtaining
### Revenant Horror
Revenant Flesh is a [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] drop from any tier of [[Revenant Horror]].
Amounts:
- Tier I: 1-3x
- Tier II: 9-18x
- Tier III: 30-50x
- Tier IV: 50-64x
- Tier V: 63-64x

|[Lv179]|[[Revenant Champion]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

|[[Revenant Viscera]]|None|32x [[Enchanted String]] [[Revenant Flesh]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) Revenant Flesh|Recipe: Revenant Flesh x32 ×4 + [[Enchanted String]] x32 → [[Revenant Viscera]]|

# Riftwart Roots
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Farming
[Riftwart Roots]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No

Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RIFTWART_ROOTS
Tooltip: Do not eat.; UNCOMMON
Riftwart Roots are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Riftwart Roots can be harvested with a [[Wand of Farming]] outside the Alchemist's in the [[West Village]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. After breaking Riftwart Roots, the Nether Wart will decrease by 1 stage, and the player's head will be rotated in a random direction. After the Riftwart Roots are broken 3 times, it will be destroyed and the player can collect it.
## Usage
The player can purchase a [[Rift Ferret Pet]] from [[Kat (Rift)]] after depositing 20 Flies, 10 Spiders and 20 Silverfish in the Vermin Bin for [[Riftwart Roots]] 12 Riftwart Roots.
Additionally, [[Riftwart Roots]] 64 (1 stack) Riftwart Roots must be thrown in the [[Alchemist (Rift)|Alchemist]]'s cauldron in order to progress in [[Detransfigured Seraphine]]'s quest.

The Ring of Coins is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Talisman of Coins]] and spawns more [[Coins]] around the player than its predecessor.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Talisman of Coins]] 4x [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]|Recipe: [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] ×4 + [[Talisman of Coins]] → Ring of Coins|
## Usage
The Ring of Coins occasionally spawns groups of [[Coins]] on the ground around the player while on public islands. Any player on the server can see and collect these coins. Picking up a group awards the player [[Coins]].
Like the [[Talisman of Coins]], the effect does not function on the [[Private Island]] or in [[The Garden]].

The Ring of Power is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Talisman of Power]] and upgrades into the [[Artifact of Power]], and is unlocked at [[Gemstone|Gemstone VI (6)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Gemstone VI (6) ([[Gemstone]])|1x [[Talisman of Power]] 1x [[Gemstone Mixture]] 7x Fine Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|Recipe: [[Gemstone]] ×7 + [[Gemstone Mixture]] + [[Talisman of Power]] → Ring of Power|
## Usage
The Ring of Power can have 4 [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] applied to it in the [[Geo|Gemstone Grinder]] at [[Geo]]. Applied Gemstones provide half of the stats they would usually give.
The following Gemstones can be placed in the Ring of Power:
- [[Gemstone|Ruby]]
- [[Gemstone|Amethyst]]
- [[Gemstone|Jade]]
- [[Gemstone|Amber]]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|

A Rusty Coin is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item obtained from [[Fishing]] while using a [[Junk Sinker]].
## Obtaining
A Rusty Coin can be obtained as a GOOD JUNK CATCH by fishing with a [[Junk Sinker]] applied to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
## Usage
### Merchants
Rusty Coins can be used to purchase various items from [[Junker Joel]].
Name: Ingredients
16x [[Treasure Bait]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 1x Rusty Coin
[[Challenging Rod]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 8x Rusty Coin 1x Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]])
Backwater Helmet ([[Backwater Armor]]): [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x Angler Helmet ([[Angler Armor]])
Backwater Chestplate ([[Backwater Armor]]): [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x Angler Chestplate ([[Angler Armor]])
Backwater Leggings ([[Backwater Armor]]): [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x Angler Leggings ([[Angler Armor]])
Backwater Boots ([[Backwater Armor]]): [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x Angler Boots ([[Angler Armor]])
[[Junk Talisman]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 32x Rusty Coin
[[Backwater Necklace]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x [[Angler Necklace]]
[[Backwater Cloak]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x [[Angler Cloak]]
[[Backwater Belt]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x [[Angler Belt]]
[[Backwater Gloves]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 1x [[Angler Bracelet]]
[[Enchanted Book]] (Luck of the Sea VI ([[Luck of the Sea]])): [[Rusty Coin]] 16x Rusty Coin
[[Hermit Crab Pet]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 32x Rusty Coin
[[Stingy Sinker]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 64x Rusty Coin
[[Bronze Ship Engine]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 64x Rusty Coin 8x [[Busted Belt Buckle]]
[[Gold Bottle Cap]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) Rusty Coin 64x [[Busted Belt Buckle]] 8x [[Old Leather Boot]]
[[Treasure Hook]]: [[Rusty Coin]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Rusty Coin 32x [[Busted Belt Buckle]] 4x [[Old Leather Boot]] 1x [[Bayou Water Orb]]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bayou Water Orb]]|None|1x [[Old Leather Boot]] 1x [[Condensed Lily Pad]] 1x [[Poison Sample]] 1x [[Water Orb]] 8x [[Alligator Skin]] 8x [[Busted Belt Buckle]] 16x [[Torn Cloth]] 16x [[Bronze Bowl]] [[Rusty Coin]] 64x Rusty Coin|Recipe: [[Busted Belt Buckle]] x8 + [[Old Leather Boot]] + [[Alligator Skin]] x8 + [[Bronze Bowl]] x16 + [[Water Orb]] + [[Torn Cloth]] x16 + [[Condensed Lily Pad]] + Rusty Coin x64 + [[Poison Sample]] → [[Bayou Water Orb]]|
|[[Geometric Oddity]]|None|1x [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] 8x [[Frogcoin]] [[Rusty Coin]] 16x Rusty Coin 32x [[Ink Sac]] 64x [[Treasure Bait]]|Recipe: [[Ink Sac]] x32 + Rusty Coin x16 + [[EroC enotS egrofeR]] + [[Frogcoin]] x8 + [[Treasure Bait]] x64 → [[Geometric Oddity]]|
|[[Hotspot Talisman]]|Clay Ball V (5) ([[Clay Ball]])|[[Rusty Coin]] 4x Rusty Coin 4x [[Enchanted Clay Ball]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Clay Ball]] ×4 + Rusty Coin ×4 → [[Hotspot Talisman]]|
|[[Drone Mod: Contraband]]|None|1x [[Old Leather Boot]] 1x [[Water Orb]] 8x [[Busted Belt Buckle]] [[Rusty Coin]] 64x Rusty Coin|Recipe: [[Old Leather Boot]] + Rusty Coin x32 ×2 + [[Water Orb]] + [[Busted Belt Buckle]] x8 → [[Drone Mod: Contraband]]|

The Salamander Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Good Karma]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Salamander Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Newt Shard]]
- Second: Water Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Salamander Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Good Karma]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Good Karma ([[Attributes]]); Grants +2–20 [[Hunting Fortune]] towards UNCOMMON Attributes.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Salamander Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Salamander Shard has the following description:
- Salamanders thrive in volcanic regions, their bodies glowing with an inner heat that can ignite their surroundings.

The Salmon Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Lost and Found]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Salmon Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Salmon]] with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]].
### Huntraps
A Salmon Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Murkwater Depths]] (Underwater) or [[Murkwater Shallows]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Salmon Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Salmon Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Lost and Found]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Lost and Found ([[Attributes]]); Grants a +0.1%–1% chance to find a [[Wet Book]] when fishing a Treasure.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Salmon Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Salmon Shard has the following description:
- Their scales can detect the slightest changes in water pressure, helping them avoid predators and obstacles.

# Salt Cube
Aliases: Salty
Categories: Uncommon items, Reforge stones, Fishing, Events, Season of Jerry
[Salt Cube]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Salty
Applied To: [[Fishing Rods]]
Apply Cost: 25,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Terry]]
Buy: 64 (1 stack) [[Hunk of Ice]]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SALT_CUBE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Salty reforge when combined with a Fishing Rod.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Sea Creature Chance; Salty Bonus This gives 2x stats while on  Jerry's Workshop.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; UNCOMMON REFORGE STONE
The Salt Cube is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Salty]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], which grants [[Sea Creature Chance]].
## Obtaining
The Salt Cube can be purchased from [[Terry]] for 64 [[Hunk of Ice]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Salt Cube can be applied to [[Fishing Rods]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Salty]] reforge, to grant [[Sea Creature Chance]], which scales with the Fishing Rod's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement.
Additionally, it grants the Salty Bonus, which doubles the stats of the reforge on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+0.5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+1.5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+2 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+3 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+4 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|+5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Salty Bonus; This gives x2 stats while on Jerry's Workshop.|Salty Bonus; This gives x2 stats while on Jerry's Workshop.|Salty Bonus; This gives x2 stats while on Jerry's Workshop.|Salty Bonus; This gives x2 stats while on Jerry's Workshop.|Salty Bonus; This gives x2 stats while on Jerry's Workshop.|Salty Bonus; This gives x2 stats while on Jerry's Workshop.|Salty Bonus; This gives x2 stats while on Jerry's Workshop.|
|Cost to apply|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|25,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- Prior to the November 30, 2022 patch, the [[Reforging|Salty]] was obtained from [[Terry]] directly, instead of being accessible via a [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]].

# Salted Sunflower Seeds
Aliases: Sunflower Seed, Sunflower Seeds, Sunflower seed, Sunflower seeds
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients
[Salted Sunflower Seeds]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SALTED_SUNFLOWER_SEEDS
Tooltip: Dropped rarely from Sunflower during the Harvest Feast event.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Salted Sunflower Seeds are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item dropped during the [[Harvest Feast]] used to craft [[Sunflower Butter]] and [[Salty Nozzle]].
## Obtaining
### Farming
Salted Sunflower Seeds has a 0.006% (1 in 18,000 crops) chance to drop when breaking mature [[Sunflower]], a 15% chance to drop from a [[Dragonfly]] and a 30% chance to drop from a [[Field Mouse]] while the [[Harvest Feast]] is ongoing and the crop is in season.
[[Overbloom]] is used to increase the drop chance.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sunflower Butter]]|None|1x [[Bowl]] 8x [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] [[Salted Sunflower Seeds]] 8x Salted Sunflower Seeds 8x [[Enchanted Packed Ice]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Blaze Powder]] x4 ×2 + Salted Sunflower Seeds x4 ×2 + [[Enchanted Packed Ice]] x4 ×2 + [[Bowl]] → [[Sunflower Butter]]|
|[[Salty Nozzle]]|None|[[Salted Sunflower Seeds]] 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) Salted Sunflower Seeds|Recipe: Salted Sunflower Seeds x16 ×6 → [[Salty Nozzle]]|
### [[Garden Visitors]]
When visiting [[The Garden]], [[Taylor]] can request [[Salted Sunflower Seeds]] 256 (4 stacks) Salted Sunflower Seeds in exchange for the [[Barn Skins|Fast Food Barn Skin]].
## History

The Scavenger Ring is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that causes all monsters to drop extra [[Coins]] when killed.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Scavenger Talisman]] 4x [[Blood-Stained Coins]] 8x [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]|Recipe: [[Blood-Stained Coins]] ×4 + [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] x2 ×4 + [[Scavenger Talisman]] → Scavenger Ring|
## Usage
Accessory:
Scavenger Ring
[Effects]
[Grants 0.6 Coins per level of the killed enemy.]
Monsters drop 0.6 [[Coins]] per level, in addition to any coins dropped without it.
This effect stacks with the [[Scavenger]] enchantment, allowing a player to receive 2.4 [[Coins]] per level with [[Scavenger|Scavenger VI]] and the Accessory.

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Seagull Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Mangrove Collector]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Mangrove Collector ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Mangrove Fortune]] for every digit in your Mangrove Log collection (Max 10M collection).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Shady Ring
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories, Dark Auction, Ring
[Shady Ring]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Crooked Artifact]]
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Lucius]]
Buy: 500,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHADY_RING
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Get 1% off on most shops.; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Shady Ring is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that gives its user 1% off on most shops.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
The Shady Ring can be purchased from [[Lucius]] for 500,000 [[Coins]] after buying 1 item from the [[Dark Auction]].
If the player loses the item, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] in the [[Gold Mine]] for 500,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Shady Ring
[Effects]
[Get 1% off on most shops.]
### Shop Purchase
The Shady Ring can be upgraded to the [[Crooked Artifact]] for 2,000,000 [[Coins]] after buying 3 [[Dark Auction]] items.
## Trivia
- The price-reducing effect of the Shady Ring works with most NPC shops in the game.
  - Notably it doesn't work for NPCs that provide a service, rather than selling items such as: [[Vanessa]], [[Maddox the Slayer]], [[Kat]], [[Fann]], [[Blacksmith]], [[Geo]], [[Jotraeline Greatforge]], [[Treasure Hunter]], analyzing mutations at [[Jake]], [[Teddy]] and [[Freddy]], [[Jacobus]], [[Zarina]] and [[Roddy]].
  - Despite being accessible by, owned by or related to NPCs, it doesn't affect [[Auction House]], [[Bazaar]], [[Dark Auction]], [[Shen's Auction]], the [[Regalia Room]], recipes that cost [[Coins]] at [[The Forge|Forge]], opening [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] or [[Richard|Stonks Auction]].
    - On the other hand, it does affect [[The Garden|Skymart]], which isn't accessed via any NPCs.
  - Still, there are certain shops that aren't affected; known exceptions include: [[Bea]], [[Fann]], the [[Golden Dragon]], [[Grumblefoot]], the [[Jade Dragon]], [[Ludleth]], [[Oringo]], [[Rosetta]], [[Seymour]], [[Jax]], [[Odger]] and [[Researcher Ribery]]
    - Oddly, Certain shops are affected only partially. It affects all items in [[Brynmor]]'s shop except [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Packrat Shard]]. Several items from [[Rusty]]'s shop aren't affected either.
  - It even works with [[Centauri]] on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], but only for costs above 1 [[Coins|Coin]], such as when trading a stack of items.
- Despite not being part of the item's description, it only affect [[Coins]] prices, and won't affect other currencies or the amounts of items needed to purchase something.
- This ring is one of 6 rings that do not have a talisman version preceding it, however directly skipping to the ring tier. Other rings with the same property are the [[Devour Ring]], the [[Bait Ring]], the [[Emerald Ring]], the [[Haste Ring]], and the [[Catacombs Expert Ring]].

- [[Lucius]]
- [[Dark Auction|Dark Auctions]]
- [[Discounts]]

|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Fixed Shady Ring not keeping recombobulation ([[Recombobulator 3000]]) when upgraded to [[Crooked Artifact]].|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed purchase requirement from 5 [[Dark Auction]] purchases to 1.|

# Shark Bait
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Craftable items, Fishing
[Shark Bait]
Type: [[Bait]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 8 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHARK_BAIT
Tooltip: Fishing Bait Consumes on Cast; Grants a +20% chance to catch Sharks during the Fishing Festival.; UNCOMMON BAIT
The Shark Bait is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Bait]] that boosts the player's chance to catch [[Sharks (disambiguation)|Sharks]] during the [[Fishing Festival]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|2x [[Shark Fin]]|Recipe: [[Shark Fin]] ×2 → Shark Bait x16|
### Seal's Treat Bag
128x Shark Bait have a 5.5% (1 in 18) chance to be obtained from a [[Seal's Treat Bag]].
## Usage
### Fishing
Shark Bait can be kept in either the player's inventory or [[Bait Sack]]. When casting a [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]], a Shark Bait will be consumed to grant a +20% chance to catch Sharks during the [[Fishing Festival]]. The effects last until the player reels in their rod. If the player doesn't catch anything when reeling, or otherwise cancels their catch, the Bait will be refunded.

|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Shark Bait as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No

Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SILVA_DOMINUS
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Damage: +10; Ability: Absorptio RIGHT CLICK This weapon lets you absorb 10% of the basic combat stats of any weapon.; Ability: Vis Temperata Each strike of this weapon has its damage capped at 20% of the enemy's max Health. This weapon cannot cause a fatal blow.; Silva vocat, Dominus respondet.; This item can be reforged!; UNCOMMON AXE
A Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Huntaxes|Huntaxe]].
## Obtaining
### Alan
A Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus can be purchased from [[Alan]] for 300,000 [[Coins]] and a [[Worn Huntaxe - Genesis]].
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap III (3) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Worn Huntaxe - Genesis]] 8x [[Stretching Sticks]] 48x [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x16 ×3 + [[Worn Huntaxe - Genesis]] + [[Stretching Sticks]] x8 → Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus|
## Usage
The Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus has two abilities:
- Absorptio allows the Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus to absorb 10% of the basic [[Stats|Combat Stats]] ([[Damage]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Damage]], etc) of any weapon.
- Vis Temperata caps the [[Damage]] of the Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus at 20% of the enemy's max health and prevents the user from killing the enemy. This allows one to easily syphon their [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] with a [[Black Holes|Pocket Black Hole]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus]]|Vinesap V (5) ([[Vinesap]])|[[Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus]] 1x Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus 16x [[Stretching Sticks]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x64 ×3 + Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus + [[Stretching Sticks]] x16 → [[Reinforced Huntaxe - Cursus]]|
## Trivia
- The text in the Huntaxe's description, "Silva vocat, Dominus respondet." is Latin, and means "The forest calls, the Lord answers."
  - The ability name "Vis Temperata" means "Tempered force".
## History

|Jul 31, 2025|Added Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus to Museum.|
|Jul 31, 2025|Added 6 [[Stretching Sticks]] to recipe.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Sharpened Huntaxe - Dominus as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Showcase Block

[Showcase Block]

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SHOWCASE_BLOCK
Tooltip: Show off an item in this fancy glass display box! RIGHT CLICK to put in or swap an item.; ❣ Requires Social Skill 10.; UNCOMMON
The Showcase Block is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Social]] block, used to display items.
## Obtaining
The Showcase Block can be purchased from [[Amelia]] for 50,000 [[Coins]], once players have reached [[Social|Social X (10)]], or be purchased from the [[Auction House]].
## Usage
Once it is placed down on the [[Private Island]], it allows the player to put any item inside. Items stored inside of Showcase Blocks rotate as if they were thrown on the ground, with the name of the item above it, however some items do not display properly and will have unknown (item type). The maximum amount of Showcase Blocks you can have on your island is 10.
WARNING You can't place it if you don't have [[Social|Social X (10)]]!
## Appearance
Showcase Blocks are also located in the [[Auction House]], [[Dark Auction]], [[The End]], and [[Sherry's Showroom]] displaying items available for purchase.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 12, 2020|0.9.12|Added Showcase Block.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Showcase Block not showing some item names and lore correctly.|
Linked from: [[Private Island]], [[Social]], [[Social/Leveling Rewards]]

## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Shrieky Tiki Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Shrieky Tiki]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]].
### Fusion
A Shrieky Tiki Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||

[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Shrieky Tiki Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Construct Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Construct Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Construct]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

# Silent Pearl
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Craftable items, Ender Pearl Collection, The End
[Silent Pearl]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Ender Pearl I (1) ([[Ender Pearl]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Pearl Dealer]]
Buy: 150 [[Coins]]
Sell: 100 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 8x [[End Stone]] 8x [[Ender Pearl]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SILENT_PEARL
Tooltip: Prevents pearl damage and Enderman from spawning when thrown on The End Island!; UNCOMMON
The Silent Pearl is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It is a special [[Ender Pearl]] that is sold by the [[Pearl Dealer]] in [[The End]]. Just like a normal [[Ender Pearl]], a Silent Pearl can be thrown and will teleport the player to the place where they land. However, players can use Silent Pearls to teleport without attracting [[Enderman|Endermen]], as they won't notice them. It is useful to get to the bottom of the [[The End]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Ender Pearl I (1) ([[Ender Pearl]])|8x [[End Stone]] 8x [[Ender Pearl]]|Recipe: [[End Stone]] ×8 + [[Ender Pearl]] x8 → Silent Pearl x8|
### Shop Purchase
Silent Pearls are sold in the [[Pearl Dealer]]'s shop. Players can purchase 8 for 1,200 [[Coins]].
### End Race
When players complete the [[End Race]] in 3 minutes for the first time, they will receive 8 Silent Pearls for free.
## Usage
Silent Pearls can be thrown like regular [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]], but they don't spawn [[Enderman|Endermen]] nor deal damage upon landing.
## Trivia
- Like normal [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]], Silent Pearls cannot be used during the [[End Race]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Silent Pearl.|
|Aug 22, 2023|0.19.3|Changed price from 1,250 [[Coins]] to 150 [[Coins]] per Silent Pearl.|
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|Silent Pearls can now be crafted.|
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|Changed rarity from RARE ([[Rarity]]) to UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]).|

# SkyMart Turbo Vacuum
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Tools
[SkyMart Turbo Vacuum]
Type: Vacuum ([[Vacuums]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Damage]]: +150; [[Farming Fortune]]: +10
[Upgrades]

Next →: [[SkyMart Hyper Vacuum]]
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SKYMART_TURBO_VACUUM
Tooltip: Damage: +150; Farming Fortune: +10; Can be used to damage  Pests.; Ability: Vacuum HOLD RIGHT CLICK Deals 150  Damage per second to nearby  Pests.; Ability: Pest Tracker LEFT CLICK Leads you in the direction of the nearest  Pest.; Cooldown: 1s; Vacuum Bag: 0 Pests Vacuum  Pests in your Garden to increase!; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires Garden Level 5.; UNCOMMON VACUUM
The SkyMart Turbo Vacuum is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Vacuums|Vacuum]].
## Obtaining
The SkyMart Turbo Vacuum can be purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 1 [[SkyMart Vacuum]] and 200 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Vacuum:
SkyMart Turbo Vacuum [[Damage]]: +150
[Effects]
[Ability: Vacuum HOLD RIGHT CLICK; Deals 150 Damage per second to nearby Pest (Pest (Mob Type))s. Ability: Pest Tracker LEFT CLICK; Leads you in the direction of the nearest Pest (Pest (Mob Type)).; Cooldown: 1s]
The SkyMart Turbo Vacuum can be used to capture [[Pests]]. When used near a pest, it will deal 150 [[Damage]] per second. [[Pests (Currency)|Pests]] captured with the SkyMart Vacuum will be sucked up, and can be exchanged at [[Pesthunter Phillip]] for temporary [[Farming Fortune]] and various goods at his shop.
Left Clicking with the SkyMart Turbo Vacuum will create a trail of particles leading to the nearest Pest.
## History

|Mar 4, 2024|Can now suck in Pests up to 7.5 blocks away.|

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: LARGE_SLAYER_SACK
Tooltip: Holds items commonly dropped by Slayers! Items you pickup go directly into your sacks.; Capacity: 2,240 per item Multiple sacks sum their capacity!; Right Click to open sack!; UNCOMMON SACK
The Slayer Sack is a [[Sacks|Sack]] unlocked by defeating [[Slayer]] bosses and used to store their basic drops. It has 3 sizes, each increasing the number of items it can hold. The different sizes are unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer II (2)]], [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer III (3)]], and [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer II (2)]]. The Small Slayer Sack can hold 2,240 of each item, the Medium Slayer Sack can hold 6,720 of each item, and the Large Slayer Sack can hold 20,160 of each item.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Small Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])|Zombie Slayer II (2) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] 8x [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] ×8 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Slayer Sack]]|
|Medium Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])|Spider Slayer III (3) ([[Spider Slayer]])|1x Small Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]) 8x [[Tarantula Silk]]|Recipe: [[Tarantula Silk]] ×8 + [[Slayer Sack]] → [[Slayer Sack]]|
|Large Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])|Wolf Slayer II (2) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|1x Medium Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]) 8x [[Golden Tooth]]|Recipe: [[Golden Tooth]] ×8 + [[Slayer Sack]] → [[Slayer Sack]]|
## Usage
The Slayer Sack can store items obtained from [[Slayer]] bosses.
|Sack|Capacity|Items|
|-|-|-|
|Small Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])|2,240|Blaze Rod Distillate ([[Distillates]]), [[Crude Gabagool]], [[Derelict Ashe]], Gabagool Distillate ([[Distillates]]), Glowstone Distillate ([[Distillates]]), [[Golden Tooth]], [[Hemobomb]], [[Hemoglass]], [[Hemovibe]], Magma Cream Distillate ([[Distillates]]), [[Molten Powder]], Nether Wart Distillate ([[Distillates]]), [[Null Atom]], [[Null Ovoid]], [[Null Sphere]], [[Raw Soulflow]], [[Revenant Catalyst]], [[Revenant Flesh]], Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Mechanic)]]), [[Spider Catalyst]], [[Tarantula Catalyst]], [[Tarantula Silk]], [[Tarantula Web]], [[Toxic Arrow Poison]], [[Twilight Arrow Poison]], [[Undead Catalyst]], [[Wolf Tooth]]|
|Medium Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])|6,720|(same as above)|
|Large Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])|20,160|(same as above)|

### Mob Drops

## Usage

The Slow and Groovy Vinyl can be used in a [[InfiniVacuum™ Hooverius]] to increase the chance of [[Slug|Slugs]] spawning.

## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv47]|[[Sludge]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Treasure Chests
Sludge Juice can be obtained from [[Treasure Chest|Treasure Chests]] in the [[Jungle]].
### Jungle Pickaxe
There is a 3% chance to obtain Sludge Juice when mining [[Hard Stone]] in the [[Jungle]] with a [[Jungle Pickaxe]].
## Usage
Sludge Juice is used in the Forge recipe for [[Gemstone Mixture]], and can be traded for various items from [[Odawa]].
### Forging
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Forging ([[The Forge]]) Recipe|Duration|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gemstone Mixture]]|[[Gemstone Mixture]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|1 [[Gemstone Mixture]] 4 Fine Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 (5 stacks) Flawed Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 (400 stacks) Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 4 Fine Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 Flawed Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 4 Fine Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 Flawed Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 4 Fine Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 320 Flawed Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 25,600 Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Sludge Juice]] 320 Sludge Juice|4 hours|
### Odawa

|-|-|-|

|[[Jungle Amulet]]|[[Jungle Amulet]]|4 [[Jungle Heart]] [[Sludge Juice]] 500 (7 stacks plus 52) Sludge Juice|
|[[Tribal Spear]]|[[Tribal Spear]]|1 [[Jungle Heart]] [[Sludge Juice]] 100 (1 stack plus 36) Sludge Juice|
|[[Jungle Pickaxe]]|[[Jungle Pickaxe]]|[[Sludge Juice]] 200 (3 stacks plus 8) Sludge Juice|
|[[Jungle Key]]|[[Jungle Key]]|[[Sludge Juice]] 100 Sludge Juice|
|[[Gemstone]]|Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])|[[Sludge Juice]] 1 Sludge Juice|
## Trivia
- [[Sludge Juice]] 48,820 (762 stacks plus 52) Sludge Juice is required to obtain one of each unique item. This does not include [[Jungle Key|Jungle Keys]], [[Gemstone|Rough Amethyst Gemstones]], additional [[Drills]] or [[Drills|Fuel Tanks]].

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Grants +4% chance to find a Chocolate Rabbit that you haven't found yet and grants +20 Chocolate per second.; Crafted in the Factory, its smoothness melts hearts and tastes like a sweet escape.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Smooth Chocolate Bar is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that increases the chance of finding unique [[Chocolate Rabbits]] by 4% and also grants +20 [[Chocolate|Chocolate per Second]]. It is the upgraded form of the [[Nibble Chocolate Stick]] and can be upgraded into the [[Rich Chocolate Chunk]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Smooth Chocolate Bar can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]], found in the [[Chocolate Factory]].

|-|-|

Smooth Chocolate Bar

It can be used to purchase its upgraded form, the [[Rich Chocolate Chunk]] from the Chocolate Shop for 1 Smooth Chocolate Bar and 2,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]].

|-|-|

# Solar Panel
Categories: Uncommon items, Minion fuels, Craftable items, Nether Quartz Collection, Minion Modifiers, Items
[Solar Panel]
Type: [[Minion Fuel]]
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Nether Quartz IX (9) ([[Nether Quartz]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 10,000 [[Coins]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 30,720x (480 stacks (30,720 items total)) [[Nether Quartz]] 3x Oak Planks ([[Planks]]) 3x [[Glass]]
Bazaar Material cost (Cost to buy all necessary materials from the Bazaar.): (not including: 3 Oak Planks ([[Planks]]), 3 [[Glass]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SOLAR_PANEL
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Increases the speed of your minion by 25% during the day. Unlimited Duration!; UNCOMMON
The Solar Panel is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Minion Fuel]] unlocked at [[Nether Quartz|Nether Quartz IX (9)]]. It can be used to speed up a [[Minions|Minion]] by 25% during the day when placed inside the Minion Fuel Slot in the Minion GUI ( Orange Glass). Unlike most other fuels, it has an unlimited duration. It is not consumed and will be returned when clicked or a minion is picked up.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Nether Quartz IX (9) ([[Nether Quartz]])|3x Oak Planks ([[Planks]]) 3x [[Glass]] 192x (3 stacks (192 items total)) [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]]|Recipe: [[Glass]] ×3 + [[Enchanted Nether Quartz]] x64 ×3 + [[Planks]] ×3 → Solar Panel|
## Usage
### Minion Fuel
Solar Panel increases minion speed by 25% during the day. It has an unlimited duration and can freely be taken out of the minion. Solar Panel can be placed in all [[Minions]].
#### Day Saver
Using it in addition to a [[Day Saver]] will cause it to give the boost permanently.
However, the Day Saver does not work when the player is offline; thus, the Solar Panel effectively provides a ~12.5% increase in minion speed when the player is not around.
A better option might be the [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]] as it provides the same 25% buff, but does not require daytime and therefore continues to provide the full buff even when the player is offline.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 26, 2019|0.4|Added Solar Panel.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Solar Panel to the [[Bazaar]].|

The Sparrow Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Fig Collector]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Fig Log VII (7) ([[Fig Log]])|16x [[Enchanted Egg]] 16x [[Enchanted Feather]] 32x [[Enchanted Fig Log]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Fig Log]] x8 ×4 + [[Enchanted Feather]] x4 ×4 + [[Enchanted Egg]] x16 → Sparrow Shard|
### Fusion
A Sparrow Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Sparrow Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Fig Collector]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Fig Collector ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1–10 [[Fig Fortune]] for every digit in your Fig Log collection (Max 10M collection).
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Sparrow Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||

[Show More]

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Sparrow Shard has the following description:
- Their keen eyes can spot the tiniest seeds, making them adept foragers.

The Spider Talisman is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] obtained by killing the [[Broodmother]] [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] in the [[Spider's Den]]. It reduces the damage taken from [[Arthropod]] mobs by 5%. It can be upgraded to the [[Spider Ring]], which can be further upgraded to the [[Spider Artifact]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv41]|[[Broodmother]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Usage
Accessory:
Spider Talisman
[Effects]
[Reduces the damage taken from Arthropod mobs by 5%.]
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Spider Ring]]|Spider Slayer I (1) ([[Spider Slayer]])|[[Spider Talisman]] 1x Spider Talisman 64x [[Tarantula Web]]|Recipe: [[Tarantula Web]] x32 ×2 + Spider Talisman → [[Spider Ring]]|
## Tips
- When trying to obtain the talisman, the [[Travel Scroll to Spider's Den Top of Nest]] allows players with the 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] rank or higher to quickly swap between lobbies while searching for a [[Broodmother]].
## Trivia
- Prior to the October 5, 2021 Update, there was a bug that allowed the [[Spider Talisman]] Spider Talisman, [[Spider Ring]], and [[Spider Artifact]] to be stacked to get a total of 30% damage reduction.

Tooltip: A magically imbued axe used by Mages to carefully harvest Brown Mushrooms in the Crimson Isle.; This item can be reforged!; UNCOMMON FARMING TOOL
The Spore Harvester is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item used to harvest [[Mushroom Spore|Mushroom Spores]].
## Obtaining
The Spore Harvester can be purchased from the [[Mage Emissary]] for 100,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
The Spore Harvester can be used to harvest [[Mushroom Spore|Mushroom Spores]] in the [[Mystic Marsh]] and [[Scarleton]], which are used to buy the [[Charminizer]] from the [[Mage Emissary]].
## Tips
- Unlike [[Red Thornleaf]], [[Mushroom Spore|Mushroom Spores]] are affected by [[Farming Fortune]]. It is therefore recommended to use gear designed for [[Farming]].
## Trivia
- Before the [[Farming Tools]] rework, the Spore Harvester was classifed as a [[Hoes|Hoe]] and able to be enchanted with [[Harvesting]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Sprawl Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Animal Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Animal Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Animal]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Sprawl Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Sprawl Shard has the following description:
- Able to glide through both the water and the skies with an eerie silence. It uses this stealth to sneak up on smaller animals that it deems its prey.

# Sprayonator
Aliases: Sprayonator Material
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Pages with UIs, Tools
[Sprayonator]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Skill Level ([[Skills]]) Requirement: Garden V (5) ([[The Garden]])
[Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Juicy Sprayonator]]
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SPRAYONATOR
Tooltip: Ability: Configure LEFT CLICK Select a nozzle and material for spraying. Certain materials attract certain types of  Pests.; Selected Nozzle: Basic Selected Material: Compost Attracts: Earthworm, Mosquito; Ability: Spray RIGHT CLICK Sprays your selected material onto the Garden plot you are on. Lasts for 30m.; Basic Nozzle Bonus:; ➜ +25 Bonus Pest Chance while in a sprayed plot.; ➜  Pests are 1.5x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.; Cooldown: 3s; ❣ Requires Garden Level 5.; UNCOMMON
Sprayonator is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Sprayonator can be claimed from [[Pesthunter Pamela]] once or purchased from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] for 25 [[Copper]].
## Usage
Sprayonator:
Sprayonator
[Effects]
[Ability: Configure LEFT CLICK; Select a nozzle and material for spraying. Certain materials attract certain types of Pest (Pest (Mob Type)). Ability: Spray RIGHT CLICK; Sprays your selected material onto the Garden plot you are on. Lasts for 30m.; ➜ +25 Bonus Pest Chance while in a sprayed plot.; ➜ Pest (Pest (Mob Type)) are 1.5x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.; Cooldown: 3s]
The Sprayonator can be used to spray a [[The Garden|Garden]] plot for 30 minutes using [[Compost]], [[Plant Matter]], [[Dung]], [[Honey Jar]], [[Tasty Cheese]], or [[Jelly]].
### Attracting Pests
The Sprayonator can be used with the appropriate materials to attract certain [[Pests]] to plots. When a spray is active, [[Pests]] are guaranteed to spawn in the plot the player is farming in, or one of the four directly adjacent plots.
The materials below can be used to attract specific pests:
|Material|Attracted Pests|
|-|-|
|[[Compost]]|[[Earthworm]], [[Mosquito]]|
|[[Plant Matter]]|[[Locust]], [[Slug]]|
|[[Dung]]|[[Beetle]], [[Fly]]|
|[[Honey Jar]]|[[Moth]], [[Cricket]]|
|[[Tasty Cheese]]|[[Rat]], [[Mite]]|
|[[Jelly]]|[[Praying Mantis]], [[Dragonfly]], [[Firefly]]|
The weights of the targeted [[Pests]] are increased by +1,000, on top of the base 100 weight.
### Nozzles
Additional effects are applied in sprayed plots depending on the selected nozzle:
- Basic Nozzle: Consumes 1 of the material. Grants +25 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] while in a sprayed plot. Pests are 1.5x more likely to spawn in sprayed plots.
## Trivia
- While the [[Bonus Pest Chance]] buff is applied whenever the player is breaking crops in a sprayed plot, the increased chance for targeted [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]] only applies if the plot they spawn in is sprayed as well.
  - Since pests can spawn either in the plot the player is farming on, or a directly adjacent plot in one of the four cardinal directions, this will result in the increased chance sometimes not being applied unless the player sprays all adjacent plots as well.
  - This has been acknowledged as a limitation due to the issue of determining what to do if the two plots are both sprayed but with different materials.

Squid Boots are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] pair of [[Boots]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv210]|[[Plhlegblast]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|

## Usage
Boots:
Squid Boots [[Health]]: +100 [[Sea Creature Chance]]: +1
[Effects]

## Trivia
- When a [[Runecrafting|rune]] is applied to it, the rune would overlap with the effect that the boots make. This effect is most prominent with a Zap Rune or Sparkling Rune.
- The Squid Boots is one of 9 armor pieces which have the darkest color with a hex color code of ⁠ #000000. The others are [[Bat Person Armor]], [[Obsidian Chestplate]], [[Tarantula Armor]], [[Shadow Assassin Armor]], [[Rancher's Boots]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor|Necromancer Lord Chestplate]], [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Wither Armor]].
- For a limited time, Squid Boots obtained within [[Dungeons]] would receive the dungeon item quality NBT tag, granting it a stat boost.

The Starter Lava Rod is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Lava [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
## Obtaining
The Starter Lava Rod is obtained by talking to [[Odger]] in the [[Crimson Isle]] at the top of the [[Blazing Volcano]]. He will give the player one for free on their first interaction. They can be bought later for 10,000 [[Coins]] from Odger.
## Usage
Fishing Rod:
Starter Lava Rod [[Damage]]: +50 [[Strength]]: +50 [[Fishing Speed]]: +10 [[Trophy Chance]]: +5
The Starter Lava Rod can be used to fish in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]] to be used.
A Starter Lava Rod with no enchantments can be used to fish up Vanille [[Trophy Fish]].
## Tips
- Unless going for the Vanille [[Trophy Fish]], it is recommended to use a [[Topaz Rod]] instead if the player has reached [[Fishing|Fishing XIX (19)]] and [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain V (5)]] but not [[Fishing|Fishing XXVII (27)]].

The Stridersurfer Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Magmatic Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Stridersurfer Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Stridersurfer]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Stridersurfer Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].
### Fusion
A Stridersurfer Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Stridersurfer Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Magmatic Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Magmatic Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Grants +3%–30% more [[Damage]] against [[Magmatic]] mobs.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Stridersurfer Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Stridersurfer Shard has the following description:
- These eerie duos combine the relentless nature of the drowned with the agility of the strider.

In case the player loses their Student's Studies, they can purchase a new one from [[Researcher Ribery]] for 250,000 [[Coins]].

# Sulphur
Aliases: Sulfur
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Pages with UIs, Mob Drops, Templates with deprecated argument names, Crafting ingredients, Sulphur Collection, Collections
[Sulphur]
Type: Collection item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: None
Next →: [[Enchanted Sulphur]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Medium Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Large Mining Sack ([[Mining Sack]]); Small Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Medium Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]); Large Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 10 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SULPHUR_ORE
Tooltip: Collection Item; &quot;Give me some Sulphur and I'll make a wand. Give me all the Sulphur and I'll conquer the world.&quot; - Unnamed Mage.; UNCOMMON ORE
Give me some Sulphur and I'll make a wand. Give me all the Sulphur and I'll conquer the world.
— Unnamed Mage
Sulphur is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] collection item and crafting ingredient, obtained from [[Ores|Sulphur Ore]] found on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Collection
Collecting Sulphur increases the player's Sulphur [[Collections|Collection]], which unlocks the recipes for certain items upon collecting certain amounts of pieces of Sulphur.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|200|[[Match-Sticks]] Recipe|+4|
|II|1,000|[[Enchanted Sulphur]] Recipe|+4|
|III|2,500|Small Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|IV|5,000|[[Totem of Corruption]] Recipe|+4|
|V|10,000|Medium Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|VI|15,000|[[Enchanted Sulphur Cube]] Recipe|+4|
|VII|25,000|[[Sulphur Bow]] Recipe|+4|
|VIII|50,000|Large Nether Sack ([[Nether Sack]]) Recipe|+4|
|IX|100,000|[[Implosion Belt]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
### Mining
After the [[Blazing Volcano]] erupts, Sulphur Ore is scattered all over the [[Crimson Isle]]. It appears as [[Sponge]], and requires tools with 8 [[Breaking Power]] to mine. Each block mined gives [[Sulphur]] 1 Sulphur, affected by [[Mining Fortune]].
### Mob Drops
Sulphur can also be obtained by killing [[Corrupted Mobs|X Corrupted X]] mobs. Each [[Corrupted Mobs|X Corrupted X]] mob will drop [[Sulphur]] 1 Sulphur.
Some mobs can additionally drop Sulphur:
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv375]|[[Chickzilla]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv203]|[[Matcho]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
### Minions
Sulphur is generated by [[Minions]] when a [[Corrupt Soil]] is applied to them.
### Fishing
Sulphur can also be caught while [[Treasure|Treasure Fishing]] in [[Crimson Isle]]:
|Item|Catch Type|5 [[Treasure Chance]]|20 [[Treasure Chance]]|
|-|-|-|-|
|32–64x Sulphur|GOOD CATCH ([[Treasure]])|0.86% (1 in 115.73 catches)|3.46% (1 in 28.93 catches)|
|2–4x Sulphur|Normal Catch|1.61% (1 in 62.11 catches)|1.36% (1 in 73.75 catches)|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enchanted Sulphur]]|Sulphur II (2)|[[Sulphur]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Sulphur|Recipe: Sulphur x32 ×7 → [[Enchanted Sulphur]]|
|[[Match-Sticks]]|Sulphur I (1)|[[Sulphur]] 1x Sulphur 1x [[Stick]]|Recipe: Sulphur + [[Stick]] → [[Match-Sticks]]|
### Quiver
Sulphur can be placed in the [[Quiver]] to be used as projectiles for the [[Sulphur Bow]].
## Trivia
- Oddly enough, Sulphur, and all Sulphur items, use the non-American spelling of the word [Sulfur].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 22, 2022|Added Sulphur.|
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Sulphur as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Sulphur.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Sulphur as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✔ Yes
Reforgeable: ✔ Yes
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SUNFLOWER_HEAD
Tooltip: Farming Fortune: +10; Ability: Sparkle RIGHT CLICK Spread happiness to someone one sparkle at a time.; Cooldown: 5s; This item can be reforged!; UNCOMMON HELMET
A Sunflower Head is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked at [[Sunflower|Sunflower II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Sunflower II (2) ([[Sunflower]])|8x [[Enchanted Sunflower]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sunflower]] ×8 → Sunflower Head|
## Usage
Helmet:
Sunflower Head [[Farming Fortune]]: +10
[Effects]
[Ability: Sparkle RIGHT CLICK; Spread happiness to someone one sparkle at a time!; Cooldown: 5s]
Clicking on other players while wearing a Sunflower Head allows the player to spread happiness at the cost of [[Health]], with the cost starting at 0% and increasing by 5% after every use. The ability cannot be used if the player does not have enough [[Health]].
After 20 uses, (when it should be 100%) the cost becomes Your Soul. Despite this, using the ability still requires the player to be at 100% [[Health]]. Once this occurs, using the ability displays a dialogue asking whether the player is certain that they want to use the ability. Upon confirming a few times, the player is killed, which displays a special death message and consumes the Sunflower Head.
## Dialogue
|Primary condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Using on a player|SMILE! You shared your happiness with <player>. :)|
|Using on a player|SMILE! You brightened <player>'s day a little bit. :)|
|Using on a player|SMILE! You sprinkled some joy to <player>. :)|
|Using on a player|SMILE! You showered <player> with rainbows. :)|
|Using on a player|SMILE! You smiled at <player> and they smiled back. :)|
|Message received by target player|SMILE! <player> shared their happiness with you! :)|
|Message received by target player|SMILE! <player> brightened your day a little bit. :)|
|Message received by target player|SMILE! <player> has sprinkled some joy your way! You feel a little happier. :)|
|Message received by target player|SMILE! <player> showered you with rainbows. :)|
|Message received by target player|SMILE! <player> smiled at you and you smiled back. :)|
|Using while cost is Your soul|# Are you sure you want to use this item? # [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [Yes]|# Are you though? # [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [Yes]|# This cannot be reversed. Are you positive? # [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [Yes]|# Last chance. I warned you. # [Use the ability] [No]|
|Selecting [Use the ability]|☠ Your soul has been consumed by happiness. You'll never truly be the same again.|
|Selecting [No] at any point|Wise choice.|
|Selecting [No] after Selecting [Use the ability]|It's too late to go back now...|
|Selecting any option after consumed the Sunflower Head|You are not ready to see true joy.|
|Selecting any option while not wearing the Sunflower Head|You must wear the Sunflower Head to continue.|
|Selecting any option while wearing a different Sunflower Head|This is not the right Sunflower Head!|
|Attempting to remove the Sunflower Head after selecting [Use the ability]|The Sunflower's smile grips your face tightly. No amount of struggling can make you tear it off!|
## Trivia
- Dying to a Sunflower Head for the first time grants the player the [[Achievements|"Too much joy" Achievement]].
- The [[Phoenix Pet]]'s Rekindle ability saves you from dying to the Sunflower Head.

[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Sunflower Head.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Sunflower Head.|

Tooltip: It's strangely sweet and somewhat sticky...; * Co-op Soulbound * UNCOMMON
Sweet Flesh is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item.
## Obtaining
Sweet Flesh can be obtained from killing [[Zombie|Zombies]] with a [[Sweet Axe]].
## Usage
[[Sweet Flesh]] 12 Sweet Flesh can be given to the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] in exchange for [[Great Spook Armor|Great Spook Boots]].
## Trivia
- Sweet Flesh can be obtained from any mob that is a zombie, such as [[Lapis Zombie|Lapis Zombies]], but not [[Slayer]] bosses/minibosses.
  - It can also be obtained from player mobs, such as [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblins]].
- It can additionally be obtained by swapping to the Sweet Axe with item abilities and Bows.
  - It can also be obtained if holding and using Witherborn ability from [[Wither Armor]] and its variants, [[Blaze Armor|Blaze]] and [[Frozen Blaze Armor]], as well as the [[Unstable Dragon Armor]]'s ability.
- To obtain a Sweet Flesh, a player must talk to [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] first.

The Sycophant Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Life Recovery]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Sycophant Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Revenant Sycophant]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Sycophant Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Sycophant Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Life Recovery]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Life Recovery ([[Attributes]]); Heals for +0.1%–1% of your max health each time you kill a mob Cooldown: 0.5s.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Sycophant Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Sycophant Shard has the following description:
- Sycophants are twisted, undead servants, their decayed forms clad in tattered remnants of finery.
## Trivia
- Despite being an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]], the Sycophant Shard does not have a [[Hunting]] level requirement.

# Tarantula Web
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Slayer, Spider Slayer
[Tarantula Web]
Type: Crafting Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]); Medium Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]); Large Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TARANTULA_WEB
Tooltip: Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
Tarantula Web is obtained by killing the [[Tarantula Broodfather]]. It is a [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] drop, and is used to make various items. It can also be dropped by [[Spider Slayer]] [[Tarantula Broodfather|minibosses]].
Many high-level items are crafted with [[Tarantula Silk]], which must itself be crafted with Tarantula Webs and [[Enchanted Flint]].
## Obtaining
### Tarantula Broodfather
Tarantula Webs are [[Drop Chance|1Guaranteed]] drops from the [[Tarantula Broodfather]]. They amount dropped varies based on the tier of the boss.
Amounts:
- Tier I: 2-4x
- Tier II: 4-8x
- Tier III: 16-32x
- Tier IV: 32-48x
- Tier V: 64x
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv342]|[[Primordial Viscount]]|8|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv251]|[[Mutant Tarantula]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv304]|[[Primordial Jockey]]|4|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv210]|[[Tarantula Beast]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv143]|[[Tarantula Vermin]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
