# Tasty Cheese
Aliases: Cheese
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Minion Modifiers, Minion fuels, Minions
[Tasty Cheese]
Also known as: Cheese
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Garden Sack]]
[Shop]
Sell: 500 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHEESE_FUEL
Tooltip: Minion Fuel; Can be spread across a Garden plot with a Sprayonator or used in Pest Traps.; Attracts: Rat, Mite; When in a minion, this increases the number of resources created by 2x for 1 hour!; UNCOMMON
Tasty Cheese is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Sprayonator]] Spray that increases the chances of [[Rat|Rats]] and [[Mite|Mites]] spawning. It also multiplies [[Minions|Minion]] resource generation by 2x for 1 hour.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv3]|[[Rat (Hub)]]|3|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv3]|[[Rat (Hub)]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[Lv1]|[[Field Mouse]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv1]|[[Beetle]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Cricket]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Dragonfly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Earthworm]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Firefly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Fly]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Locust]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mite]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Mosquito]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Moth]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Praying Mantis]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Rat]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
|[Lv1]|[[Slug]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|10%* ([[Pests]])|
### Rat Pet
Tasty Cheese has a chance to spawn when the player is riding a [[Rat Pet]].
### Farming
Tasty Cheese can also be obtained from the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Mooshroom Cow Pet]]'s Bovine Blessing perk, up to a 0.02% (1 in 5000) chance when farming crops.
### Attributes
[[Attributes|Visitor Chesse]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants up to 2.5% chance for [[Garden Visitors|Visitors]] to bring Tasty Cheese, which can be further increased with [[Attributes|Queenly Echo]] attribute.
[[Attributes|Sprayonator Serendipity]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants 10% chance for [[Pests]] to drop an extra [[Sprayonator|Sprayonator Material]].
## Usage
### Attracting Pests
Tasty Cheese can be used with a [[Sprayonator]] to attract [[Rat|Rats]] and [[Mite|Mites]] to a sprayed plot.
Tasty Cheese can also be used as bait in a [[Pest Trap]], [[Mouse Trap]] or [[Vermin Trap]], allowing them to catch [[Pests]], and increasing the chance to catch a [[Rat]] or a [[Mite]] by 12x.
### Minion Fuel
When used as [[Minion Fuel]], Tasty Cheese multiplies the amount of resources generated by the minion by 2x for 1 hour. Tasty Cheese can be placed in all [[Minions]].
### Great Spook
1 [[Great Spook Ring]], 2 [[Bread]], 1 [[Cooked Beef|Steak]], 1 [[Decorations|Onion]], 1 [[Decorations|Lettuce]], [[Tasty Cheese]] 1 Tasty Cheese and 1 [[French Fries]] can be used to buy a [[Great Spook Artifact]] from the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]].
## Tips
- Tasty Cheese is useful as a cheap minion fuel alternative to [[Catalyst]] or the [[Hyper Catalyst]].
## Trivia
- Tasty Cheese was added as part of YouTuber Swavy's mayor perk, due to his position as a [[Resistance General]].
- Tasty Cheese doubles the resources [[Minions]] produce, meaning it doesn't have an impact on time between actions.
- Spreading upgrades (e.g. [[Diamond Spreading]] and [[Sleepy Hollow]]) will apply to the extra drops gained from Tasty Cheese's bonus.

# Tasty Mithril
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Dwarven Mines
[Tasty Mithril]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Dwarven Mines]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MITHRIL_GOURMAND
Tooltip: Perfectly round and appetizing sphere of Mithril.; UNCOMMON
Tasty Mithril is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be obtained during the [[Dwarven Mines|Mithril Gourmand]] event.
## Obtaining
Tasty Mithril has a 30% chance to drop from [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]] during the [[Dwarven Mines|Mithril Gourmand]] event in specific areas of the [[Dwarven Mines]]. The area that players need to mine in is announced at the beginning of the event and is displayed in the sidebar.
## Usage
Tasty Mithril can be fed to [[Don Expresso]] during the [[Dwarven Mines|Mithril Gourmand]] event. Doing so will grant various rewards to the player.
## Trivia
- Feeding [[Don Expresso]] Tasty Mithril for the first time will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"A Good Review" Achievement]].
- If [[Don Expresso]] is fed Tasty Mithril from a previous event, it will be wiped from the player's inventory without counting toward the event, with the following dialogue:
[NPC] Don Expresso: THIS OLD MITHRIL IS STALE! DON'T EVER FEED THIS TO ME AGAIN!
# Tasty Mithril pieces were removed from a different event!
- It is possible to loot share Tasty Mithril using a [[Rat Pet]], however Tasty Mithril obtained this way will not contain an event UUID and therefore will be unusable.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Tasty Mithril.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Tasty Mithril.|

Item ID: TELEPORT_PAD
Tooltip: Places a Teleport Pad down, allowing you to teleport to any other Teleport Pad on your private island!; Maximum of 56 per island.; UNCOMMON
The Teleport Pad is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Block that looks like an End Portal Frame with the enchantment glint. It can be used as an easy way to travel to another pad anywhere in your island without a [[Grappling Hook]] or just by walking.
It can be placed only on your own island, but you can still use pads on other player's islands. There is a limit of 56 Teleport Pads that can be placed on the player's island. Users that are not part of the profile or co-op cannot right-click the pad.
## Obtaining
### Trades
|Requirement|Trade Input|Trade Output|
|-|-|-|
|Ender Pearl VII (7) ([[Ender Pearl]])|[[Enchanted Eye of Ender]] →|→ Teleport Pad x2|
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Instructions
Old Teleport Pad settings menu
Old color settings for Teleport Pad
The Teleport Pad menu has 4 options:
|Description|
|-|
|Set destination. User is able to see the icon, icon name, and custom name (if any) of each Teleport Pad in the selector menu.|
|Set direction a player faces upon arrival|
|Set a unique pad icon and icon name for the Teleport Pad|
|Set a custom name for the Teleport Pad|
|Pick up Teleport Pad|
### Available Icons
There are 56 available Icons. These includes:
|Images|Names|
|-|-|
|Red Wool.png Red Wool.png Yellow Wool.png Lime Wool.png Blue Wool.png Magenta Wool.png Orange Wool.png Gray Wool.png White Wool.png Green Wool.png Light Blue Wool.png Purple Wool.png Black Wool.png Cyan Wool.png|Red, Yellow, Lime, Blue, Magenta, Orange, Gray, White, Green, Aqua, Purple, Black, Cyan|
|Red Hardened Clay.png Red Hardened Clay.png Yellow Hardened Clay.png Lime Hardened Clay.png Blue Hardened Clay.png Magenta Hardened Clay.png Orange Hardened Clay.png Gray Hardened Clay.png White Hardened Clay.png Green Hardened Clay.png Light Blue Hardened Clay.png Purple Hardened Clay.png Black Hardened Clay.png Cyan Hardened Clay.png|One, Two, Three, Four, Five, Six, Seven, Eight, Nine, Ten, Eleven, Twelve, Thirteen|
|Rose Red.png Rose Red.png Dandelion.png Lime Dye.png Lapis Lazuli.png Magenta Dye.png Orange Dye.png Light Gray Dye.png Bone Meal.png Cactus Green.png Light Blue Dye.png Purple Dye.png Gray Dye.png Cyan Dye.png|Fourteen, Fifteen, Sixteen, Seventeen, Eighteen, Nineteen, Twenty, TwentyOne, TwentyTwo, TwentyThree, TwentyFour, TwentyFive, TwentySix|
|Red Stained Glass.png Red Stained Glass.png Yellow Stained Glass.png Lime Stained Glass.png Blue Stained Glass.png Magenta Stained Glass.png Orange Stained Glass.png Gray Stained Glass.png White Stained Glass.png Green Stained Glass.png Light Blue Stained Glass.png Purple Stained Glass.png Black Stained Glass.png Cyan Stained Glass.png|TwentySeven, TwentyEight, TwentyNine, Thirty, ThirtyOne, ThirtyTwo, ThirtyThree, ThirtyFour, ThirtyFive, ThirtySix, ThirtySeven, ThirtyEight, ThirtyNine|

### Available Directions
There are 9 available direction settings. These includes:
- Player's Last Direction
- Facing (8): North, North East, East, South East, South, South West, West, North West
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Teleporter Pill]]|Blaze Slayer IV (4) ([[Blaze Slayer]])|2x [[Inferno Vertex]] 4x [[Molten Powder]] [[Teleport Pad]] 64x Teleport Pad 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Enchanted Sugar]]|Recipe: [[Molten Powder]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Sugar]] x64 ×2 + [[Inferno Vertex]] ×2 + Teleport Pad x64 → [[Teleporter Pill]]|
## Trivia
- There are 111 possible unique combinations of colors available in the Teleport Pad.
- There are ten locations in the world where teleport pads are found that are not on your private island: in the [[Community Center]] leading to the [[Election Room]], in the Election Room leading back to the Community Center during the [[Mayor Election]], to access the [[Security Hall]], in [[Marco|Marco the Painter]]'s Flower House in the [[Hub Island]], leading to the [[Canvas Room]], from the Canvas Room to the Flower House, the [[Dragon's Nest]], leading between the Obsidian Rock and the Spire, along with one leading to the peak, another to the end spire, [[The End]], leading to the [[Void Sepulture]], and one to the [[Snowball Fight Cave]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 9, 2019|0.6|Added Teleport Pad.|
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added option to set the direction you are facing.|
|Dec 3, 2019|Teleport Pads can now be named.|
|Jun 22, 2022|Increased Teleport Pad limit from 12 to 28.|
|Mar 28, 2023|0.18.2|Increased Teleport Pad limit from 28 to 56.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Fixed Teleport Pad bulk trade options in [[Trades]] menu.|
|May 22, 2023|Disabled Teleport Pads on [[The Garden]].|

# Test Bucket Please Ignore
Aliases: Test Bucket Please Ignore (Outside)
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories
[Test Bucket Please Ignore]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[True Defense]]: 0.5
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TEST_BUCKET_PLEASE_IGNORE
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Was it all a test?; * Soulbound * X Rift-Transferable X UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Test Bucket Please Ignore is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Test Bucket Please Ignore can be claimed after completing the fourth puzzle in the [[Mirrorverse]] in hard mode. This mode can be activated by pressing the Wooden Button found at the start of the puzzle.
## Usage
Accessory:
Test Bucket Please Ignore (Outside) ([[Test Bucket Please Ignore]]) [[True Defense]]: +0.5
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Test Bucket Please Ignore can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]]. The Test Bucket Please Ignore does not grant stats while within the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Trivia
- This item's name may be a reference to the EVE Online alliance called Test Alliance Please Ignore, or the achievement "Test Achievement Please Ignore" from The Stanley Parable.
  - The name may also be a reference to an unimplemented item called Test Item Plz Ignore, which was added to the [[SkyBlock API]] on February 9, 2022 and removed later that same day.
## History

|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|

### Huntraps

### Fusion

# Thornshade
Categories: Uncommon items, Items in Sacks, Items, Crafting ingredients, Mutations
[Thornshade]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: THORNSHADE
Tooltip: A rose with thick vines and thorns sharper than they should be.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; UNCOMMON MUTATION
A Thornshade is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Thornshade can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 4 [[Wild Rose]] and 4 [[Veilshroom]] within 1 block of farmland.
While growing, a Thornshade needs water and has 8 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it spreads buffs and debuffs to adjacent crops.
Walking into a Thornshade deals damage similar to a [[Cactus]].
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Thornshades also drop 190 (2 stacks plus 62) [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]], 190 [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]], 800 (12 stacks plus 32) [[Wild Rose]], and +3,200 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Thornshade can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 80,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 40 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Thornshade grants an additional 500 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Thornshades are used to create a [[Chloronite]] and [[Snoozling]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Potted Cactus]]|None|1x [[Enchanted Cactus]] 1x [[Enchanted Clay Block]] [[Thornshade]] 1x Thornshade 2x [[Cactus Flower]]|Recipe: Thornshade + [[Cactus Flower]] ×2 + [[Enchanted Cactus]] + [[Enchanted Clay Block]] → [[Potted Cactus]] x60|
|[[Blooming Thorns]]|None|[[Thornshade]] 2x Thornshade 4x [[Thorn Fragment]] 18x [[Cactus Flower]] 18x [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|Recipe: [[Cactus Flower]] x6 ×3 + [[Thorn Fragment]] x2 ×2 + Thornshade x2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x6 ×3 → [[Blooming Thorns]]|
## Gallery
- Thornshade in the Greenhouse Thornshade in the [[Greenhouse]]

# Timite
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Crafting ingredients, Timite Collection, Collections
[Timite]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Material Tiers]
← Previous: [[Youngite]]
Next →: [[Obsolite]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell (Motes): 100 [[Motes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TIMITE
Tooltip: Collection Item; Older, wiser, but refusing to admit it's just a phase.; Right-click to view recipes!; RARE
Timite is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] [[Collections|Collection]] Item.
## Collection
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|25|[[Time Gun]] Recipe; [[Eon Pickaxe]] Recipe|+4|
|II|50|Time Pocket Bag ([[Time Pocket]]) Upgrade|+4|
|III|100|[[Chrono Pickaxe]] Recipe|+4|
|IV|250|[[Highlite]] Recipe|+4|
|V|500|[[Satelite]] Recipe|+4|
|VI|750|[[Discrite]] Recipe|+4|
## Obtaining
Timite can be obtained by using a [[Time Gun]] on [[Youngite]] for 2 seconds until it turns blue, then breaking it with an [[Eon Pickaxe]] or [[Chrono Pickaxe]]. Only 1 Timite will drop.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Discrite]]|Timite IV (4)|64x [[Youngite]] [[Timite]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) Timite 256x [[Obsolite]]|Recipe: [[Obsolite]] x64 ×4 + Timite x64 ×4 + [[Youngite]] x64 → [[Discrite]]|
|[[Chrono Pickaxe]]|Timite III (3)|1x [[Eon Pickaxe]] [[Timite]] 64x Timite|Recipe: Timite x16 ×4 + [[Eon Pickaxe]] → [[Chrono Pickaxe]]|
|[[Highlite]]|Timite IV (4)|16x [[Obsolite]] [[Timite]] 32x Timite 32x [[Youngite]]|Recipe: [[Youngite]] x16 ×2 + Timite x16 ×2 + [[Obsolite]] x16 → [[Highlite]]|
|[[Satelite]]|Timite V (5)|1x [[Highlite]] 32x [[Obsolite]] [[Timite]] 32x Timite 64x [[Youngite]]|Recipe: [[Youngite]] x16 ×4 + Timite x16 ×2 + [[Obsolite]] x16 ×2 + [[Highlite]] → [[Satelite]]|
### NPC Trading
[[Timite]] 20 Timite can be used to purchase [[Shen's Ringalia]] from [[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X]].
|Name|Requirements|Ingredients|
|-|-|-|
|[[Shen's Ringalia]]|Completion of Eleanor's Puzzle|25,000 [[Motes]] 64x [[Puff Crux]] 20x [[Youngite]] [[Timite]] 20x Timite 20x [[Obsolite]] 1x [[Disinfestor Gloves]]|
### Obsolite
By using a [[Time Gun]], players can turn a Timite ore into [[Obsolite]].
## Trivia
- Mining [[Timite]] Timite gives back +2 [[Rift Time]] to the player.
  - Mining [[Youngite]] or [[Obsolite]], meanwhile, gives no [[Rift Time]]. It is unknown if this is intentional or a bug.
## Gallery
- An example of a Timite vein
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added Timite.|

# Tiny Dancer
Aliases: Tiny Dancer (Outside)
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories
[Tiny Dancer]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

[Item Metadata]

Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; * Soulbound * X Rift-Transferable X UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Tiny Dancer is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]]. It is [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]].
## Obtaining
The Tiny Dancer can be claimed after fully completing the sixth puzzle in the [[Mirrorverse]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Tiny Dancer (Outside) ([[Tiny Dancer]]) [[Crit Damage]]: +1
### Transfer
Due to it being [[Rift Dimension|# Rift-Transferable #]], the Tiny Dancer can be transferred to the [[Hub Island|Hub]] through the Ender Chest next to [[Inverted Sirius]].
## Trivia
- The Tiny Dancer's name and texture are a reference to English singer, pianist and composer Sir Elton John and his song Tiny Dancer.
- Since [[Crit Damage]] does not apply while in the [[Rift Dimension]], the Tiny Dancer is only effective when outside the Rift.

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Fig Log I (1) ([[Fig Log]])|4x [[Stretching Sticks]] 64x [[Fig Log]]|Recipe: [[Fig Log]] x16 ×4 + [[Stretching Sticks]] ×4 → Tiny Scaffolding|
## Usage
### Ability
Using Tiny Scaffolding places a 2 block tall scaffold that the player can use while on [[Galatea]]. Tiny Scaffolding can be used twice, totaling 4 blocks. It lasts infinitely as long as the player is within 5 blocks.

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TITANIUM_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Mining Speed: +15; Accessory Power: +5; ❣ Requires Heart of the Mountain Tier 2.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
The Titanium Talisman is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that grants 15 [[Mining Speed]].
## Obtaining
The Titanium Talisman can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 2 [[Refined Titanium]], and 14 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Titanium Talisman]] 1 Titanium Talisman
  - 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 (80 stacks) [[Titanium]]
## Usage
### Forging
The Titanium Talisman, and 6 [[Refined Titanium]] can be used to forge a [[Titanium Ring]] once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain III (3)]]. It takes 20 hours to forge.
#### Forging Tree
- 1 [[Titanium Ring]]
  - 6 [[Refined Titanium]] 96 (1 stack plus 32) [[Enchanted Titanium]] 15,360 (240 stacks) [[Titanium]]
  - [[Titanium Talisman]] 1 Titanium Talisman 2 [[Refined Titanium]] 32 [[Enchanted Titanium]] 5,120 [[Titanium]]
## Trivia
- Upgrading this accessory will remove all [[Enrichments]] and [[Recombobulator 3000|Recombobulator 3000s]] when it is used in the Forge to obtain the next version of the accessory.
- The Titanium Talisman, [[Titanium Ring]], [[Titanium Artifact]] and [[Titanium Relic]] used to stack for a total of 150 [[Mining Speed]].
  - This has been patched.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Titanium Talisman.|
|Unknown Date|Buffed the Titanium Talisman from 10 [[Mining Speed]] to 15 [[Mining Speed]].|
|Oct 5, 2021|Fixed the Titanium Talisman, Titanium Ring, Titanium Artifact and Titanium Relic stacking.|

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bayou Water Orb]]|None|1x [[Old Leather Boot]] 1x [[Condensed Lily Pad]] 1x [[Poison Sample]] 1x [[Water Orb]] 8x [[Alligator Skin]] 8x [[Busted Belt Buckle]] [[Torn Cloth]] 16x Torn Cloth 16x [[Bronze Bowl]] 64x [[Rusty Coin]]|Recipe: [[Busted Belt Buckle]] x8 + [[Old Leather Boot]] + [[Alligator Skin]] x8 + [[Bronze Bowl]] x16 + [[Water Orb]] + Torn Cloth x16 + [[Condensed Lily Pad]] + [[Rusty Coin]] x64 + [[Poison Sample]] → [[Bayou Water Orb]]|

The Totem of Corruption's recipe is unlocked at [[Sulphur|Sulphur IV (4)]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TOXIC_ARROW_POISON
Tooltip: Consumed on arrow shot Arrows deal an additional 10% of damage as poison and reduce healing by 24% over 4 seconds.; UNCOMMON ARROW POISON
Toxic Arrow Poison is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Arrow Poison]] that is an [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from the [[Tarantula Broodfather]].
## Obtaining
Toxic Arrow Poison is an [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from Tier II+ [[Tarantula Broodfather|Tarantula Broodfathers]].
Drop Chances / Amounts:
- Tier II: 15.25% (16x)
- Tier III: 14.85% (24-30x)
- Tier IV: 14.97% (60-64x)
## Usage
[[Arrows]] deal +10% [[Damage]] and reduce [[Mob]] healing by 24% over 4 seconds. The item is consumed on arrow shot. It can also be sold to an [[NPC]] for 2,000 [[Coins]] each.
It can be stored in the [[Quiver]].
## Trivia
- It was the only [[Arrow Poison]] until the 0.11.5 Update.
- This item does not work together with [[Infinite Quiver]].
  - With [[Infinite Quiver|Infinite Quiver X]], it is always consumed.
  - It does not work with the [[Skeleton Master Armor|Skeleton Master Chestplate]]'s passive.

The Training Dummy is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item unlocked at [[Pumpkin|Pumpkin V (5)]]. It can be used to place a dummy that can be used to test out [[Weapons]] and [[Enchantments]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Pumpkin V (5) ([[Pumpkin]])|1x Armor Stand (Furniture) ([[Furniture]]) 1x [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] 224x [[Sponge]]|Recipe: [[Sponge]] x32 ×7 + [[Enchanted Pumpkin]] + [[Furniture]] → Training Dummy|
## Usage
The Training Dummy can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]]. The form and [[Mob Types]] of the dummy can be changed by right-clicking it:
|Mob|Mob Type(s)|
|-|-|
|[[Blaze]]|Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]])|
|[[Cave Spider]]|[[Arthropod]]|
|[[Creeper]]|Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]])|
|[[Enderman]]|[[Ender]]|

All mobs have 2B [[Health]] [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]. Upon dealing damage to the dummy, a DPS Meter will appear above its health bar.
Killing the dummy does not give mob drops. The dummy respawns after a short period of time.
Only up to 4 Training Dummy's can be placed on the player's [[Private Island]]. Each Training Dummy must be placed at least 5 blocks away from another Training Dummy.

# Training Weights
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Catacombs, Dungeons, Aging Items
[Training Weights]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: TRAINING_WEIGHTS
Tooltip: The longer you hold this in your inventory, the stronger you'll become for 2 minutes after removing it from your inventory!; Max +50.; Time Held: 0 Minutes Strength Gain: +1; UNCOMMON DUNGEON ITEM
Training Weights are an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that can be obtained from the [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], via [[Dungeon Secrets]].
## Usage
Training Weights will give a player additional available [[Strength]] the longer it stays in their [[Player|inventory]]. When the player removes it from their inventory, or if it is dropped or moved to any storage without shift clicking, it will shatter. The player will gain the [[Strength]], which will last for 2 minutes. Up to 50 of this effect can be stacked.
When the Training Weights shatter, it is unrecoverable.
Training Weights are a 1 time use item. If the player switches lobbies, the [[Strength]] bonus will be removed, regardless of how much time has passed.
## Tips
- Successfully completing the [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Quiz Puzzle]] in the Dungeons maxes out any Training Weights in everyone's inventory.
- Training Weights can be used as a [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret]] for the [[Secret I Potion]].

The Treasure Hoarder Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Dwarven Training]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Treasure Hoarder Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Treasure Hoarder]] with a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Huntraps
A Treasure Hoarder Shard can be obtained from [[Huntraps]] by placing them in the [[Dwarven Mines]] (Underwater).
### Fusion
A Treasure Hoarder Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Treasure Hoarder Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Dwarven Training]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Dwarven Training ([[Attributes]]); Gain +1%–10% more Heart of the Mountain Exp.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

Twilight Arrow Poison is an [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from Tier II+ [[Voidgloom Seraph|Voidgloom Seraphs]].

- Tier II: 15.15% (16x)
- Tier III: 13.41% (24-32x)

Arrows apply a Poison that increases all incoming damage by 10% for 20s. The item is consumed on arrow shot.

An Uncommon Fighting Booster Upgrade is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Boosters|Booster]]. It increases [[Damage]] against Foraging Island creatures when applied to [[Foraging]] gear.
## Obtaining
An Uncommon Fighting Booster Upgrade can be purchased from [[Maxim]] for 1 [[Figstone]], 1 [[Mangcore]], and 1 [[Helixis]].
## Usage
A Uncommon Fighting Booster Upgrade can be applied to [[Foraging]] gear in an [[Anvil]] to gain [[Damage]]. [[Common Fighting Booster]] is required to be applied[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and only one can be applied per piece. The [[Damage]] gained depends on type:
- [[Equipment]] gains 2% [[Damage]]
- [[Armor]] gains 5% [[Damage]]
- [[Axes]] gain 10% [[Damage]]
## History

# Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade
Aliases: Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster
Categories: Uncommon items, Items
[Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: FORAGING_FORTUNE_BOOSTER_UNCOMMON
Tooltip: Booster Combinable in Anvil; Apply to an item with a Common Foraging Fortune Booster to upgrade it to an Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster, which increases the amount of  Foraging Fortune it gives.; UNCOMMON BOOSTER
An Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Boosters|Booster]]. It grants [[Foraging Fortune]] when applied to [[Foraging]] gear.
## Obtaining
An Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade can be purchased from [[Maxim]] for 1 [[Figstone]], 1 [[Mangcore]], and 1 [[Helixis]].
## Usage
A Uncommon Foraging Fortune Booster Upgrade can be applied to [[Foraging]] gear in an [[Anvil]] to gain [[Foraging Fortune]]. [[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]] is required to be applied, and only one can be applied per piece. The [[Foraging Fortune]] gained depends on type:
- [[Equipment]] gains 5 [[Foraging Fortune]]
- [[Armor]] gains 10 [[Foraging Fortune]]
- [[Axes]] gain 20 [[Foraging Fortune]]
## History

Collection ([[Collections]]): Vinesap II (2) ([[Vinesap]])
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Abysmal Lasso]]
Next →: [[Entangler Lasso]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Mangrove Log]] 17x [[Vinesap]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VINERIP_LASSO
Tooltip: Hunting Tool; Ability: Throw RIGHT CLICK Throw the Lasso at Forest creatures and hold them still until they lose all their stamina.; Ability: Reel In RIGHT CLICK Reel in creatures caught by the Lasso each time their stamina depletes.;  Critters lose stamina 20% faster.; UNCOMMON LASSO
A Vinerip Lasso is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Lassos|Lasso]] whose recipe is unlocked at [[Vinesap|Vinesap II (2)]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Vinesap II (2) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] 1x [[Abysmal Lasso]] 12x [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x4 ×3 + [[Abysmal Lasso]] + [[Enchanted Mangrove Log]] → Vinerip Lasso|
## Usage
A Vinerip Lasso can be thrown at forest creatures to catch them. While held, a stamina bar appears above its head. When the bar reaches 20%[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], a prompt to reel appears. Right-clicking will deplete the stamina bar and pull in the mob.
A Vinerip Lasso depletes stamina 20% faster.
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Entangler Lasso]]|Vinesap IV (4) ([[Vinesap]])|1x [[Mangcore]] [[Vinerip Lasso]] 1x Vinerip Lasso 96x (1 stack plus 32 (96 in total)) [[Vinesap]]|Recipe: [[Vinesap]] x32 ×3 + Vinerip Lasso + [[Mangcore]] → [[Entangler Lasso]]|

# VIP Voter's Badge
Categories: Uncommon items, Accessories
[VIP Voter's Badge]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
Source: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]) reward as [[Garden Visitors]]
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Voter's Badge]]
Next →: [[Elite Voter's Badge]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Shop]
Sell: 2,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: VOTER_BADGE_VIP
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +5; Grants +6 votes in Mayor Elections.; * Co-op Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; UNCOMMON ACCESSORY
VIP Voter's Badge is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
While a mayoral candidate is elected as [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], they have a chance to appear as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]]. A VIP Voter's Badge will be rewarded to the player after accepting the offer of 3 unique mayor visitors.
If lost, the VIP Voter's Badge can be repurchased at [[Rusty]] for 35,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
The VIP Voter's Badge grants +6 votes in [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] while in the player's [[Accessory Bag]].
## Trivia
- The VIP Voter's Badge cannot have its rarity upgraded using a [[Recombobulator 3000]].
- Voter's Badge progress is on a per-player basis, meaning a player accepting a mayor visitor will not count towards their co-op members' progress.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added VIP Voter's Badge.|
|Jan 26, 2026|Fixed all mayor visitors counting towards Voter's Badge accessory line progress instead of only unique ones.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added VIP Voter's Badge.|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Fixed all mayor visitors counting towards Voter's Badge accessory line progress instead of only unique ones.|

Tooltip: Hunter's Fang I Your Hunting Axes gain +1% more stats from the weapon they hold.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 5.; UNCOMMON COMBAT SHARD (ID U21)
The Viper Shard is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Hunter's Fang]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Viper Shard can be purchased from [[Kiara]] for 100,000 [[Coins]]. The player can purchase up to 10 per day, or up to 11 with her [[Abiphones|Abiphone Contact]] unlocked.
### Fusion
A Viper Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Cuboa Shard]]
- Second: Combat Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Viper Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Hunter's Fang]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Hunter's Fang ([[Attributes]]); Your Hunting Axes gain +1%–10% more stats from the weapon they hold.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|2|3|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|3|6|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|4|10|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|5|15|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|6|21|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|7|28|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|8|36|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|12|48|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|16|64|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Viper Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Viper Shard has the following description:
- Vipers can reach lengths of up to 2 meters.

The Voidwalker Katana is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It is unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer I (1)]] and upgrades into the [[Voidedge Katana]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Enderman Slayer I (1) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|1x [[Stick]] 32x [[Null Sphere]]|Recipe: [[Null Sphere]] x16 ×2 + [[Stick]] → Voidwalker Katana|
## Usage
Sword:
Voidwalker Katana [[Damage]]: +105 [[Strength]]: +40 [[Crit Damage]]: +15%
[Effects]
[Deal +150% damage to Endermen. Receive 3% less damage from Endermen when held.]

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Voidedge Katana]]|Enderman Slayer III (3) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|[[Voidwalker Katana]] 1x Voidwalker Katana 1x [[Null Ovoid]] 1x [[Refined Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Refined Mithril]] + [[Null Ovoid]] + Voidwalker Katana → [[Voidedge Katana]]|

# W Logo Fragment
Aliases: S Logo Fragment
Categories: Uncommon items, Items, Crafting ingredients
[W Logo Fragment]
Type: Item
Rarity: UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: S_LOGO_FRAGMENT
Tooltip: Even wizards can have branding failures.; Right-click to view recipes!; UNCOMMON
W Logo Fragment is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] crafting ingredient used to upgrade the [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]] to the [[W Logo]].
## Obtaining
[[W Logo Fragment]] 1 W Logo Fragment can be obtained by beating [[Wizardman]] in the [[Wizardman|Wizard Brawl]] trial.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[W Logo]]|None|1x [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]] [[W Logo Fragment]] 4x W Logo Fragment|Recipe: W Logo Fragment ×4 + [[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]] → [[W Logo]]|
## Trivia
- [[W Logo]], and by extension W Logo Fragment, is a likely reference to Superman logo, which is probably why they were called S Logo, and S Logo Fragment, for couple hours after their release.
  - The items are still referred to with S in their IDs.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added S Logo Fragment.|
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Renamed S Logo Fragment to W Logo Fragment|
